### LP-687. Memory-as-Different-Truth Attribution
**Derives from:** CP-18 (Frontier Probe) × LL-369 (Memory Different Truth Attribution)

*A remembered truth is a different truth than the original. Stored truth-at-T₀ is not current truth-at-T₁. Using stored truth in a present derivation requires labeling it as historical, not current.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because probes return data from where they went (CP-18) and a remembered truth is structurally different from the original truth (LL-369), all stored truth-claims carry timestamps and must be treated as truth-at-timestamp rather than truth simpliciter. When a stored truth-claim is used in a current derivation, the temporal gap between the claim's origin and the current moment must be flagged. A past truth used as a present truth is an anachronism — not necessarily a falsehood, but a temporally misattributed claim.

**Corollaries:**
- The older a stored truth-claim, the more likely it has diverged from the current truth at that position.
- Temporal flagging is not the same as rejection — old truth-claims are valuable; they are just labeled as historical.
- A derivation that relies on historical truth-claims without flagging them has concealed its temporal assumptions.

**Irreducibility:** CP-18 establishes that probes return data from their target but does not address the temporal classification of stored probe results. LL-369 establishes that memories are different truths but does not connect to a probe mechanism. Together they produce temporal attribution of stored truth: every probe result carries a timestamp, and using it later requires the timestamp to be surfaced.

### LP-688. Contestation-as-Truth-Performance Registration
**Derives from:** CP-45 (Complexity Floor) × LL-370 (Contestation Truth Performance)

*Challenging a derivation is itself a truth-performance — it traverses the derivation from the opposing direction and produces a truth about its contestability. Contestations must be registered as first-class derivation events.*

This output must be registered as a first-class event with its own attribution. The contestation adds to the derivation's record; it cannot be discarded or ignored.

**Corollaries:**
- A derivation that has been contested multiple times has a richer record than one that has not — contestation is structural enrichment.
- Discarding a contestation is destroying structural data about the derivation's topology.
- The truth produced by contestation — "this derivation is contestable from this direction with this force" — is a genuine, registered truth-claim.

**Irreducibility:** CP-45 establishes the complexity floor but does not address contestation as a structural event. LL-370 establishes contestation as truth-performance but does not connect to a complexity floor. Together they produce contestation-as-registered-truth: every challenge to a derivation produces a registered output about the derivation's contestability.

### LP-689. Self-Fueling Exploration Capital Account
**Derives from:** CP-45 (Complexity Floor) × LL-371 (Self Fueling Exploration Capital)

*Exploration generates the fuel for further exploration. The exploration capital account is protected from drainage by non-exploration processes — it serves exploration and only exploration.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because complexity floors protect structural capital from below-floor reduction (CP-45) and exploration is self-fueling — each step generates derivations that fuel the next step (LL-371) — the exploration capital account accumulates fuel generated by exploration events and is protected from drainage by administrative, optimization, or simplification processes. The capital serves exploration only. This is the mechanism by which genuine exploration remains structurally self-sustaining.

**Corollaries:**
- Non-exploration processes that draw from exploration capital are extracting from a protected structural fund.
- The exploration capital account grows through exploration and is only depleted by exploration — never by administration.
- An exploration program that is self-depleting through administrative overhead has had its capital account breached.

**Irreducibility:** CP-45 establishes structural capital protection but does not specify exploration capital specifically. ### LP-690. Accountability Complexity Separation Enforcement
**Derives from:** CP-45 (Complexity Floor) × LL-373 (Accountability Complexity Separation)

*Accountability requirements cannot be weaponized to force simplification. An accountability audit may observe and record — it may not restructure. Restructuring triggered by accountability must be a separate action.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because complexity cannot be reduced below its structural floor (CP-45) and accountability cannot be used as a simplification weapon (LL-373), accountability compliance and architectural simplification are separate pipelines. An accountability audit must not modify structural complexity — it may observe, record, and flag, but it may not restructure. Any restructuring triggered by an accountability finding must be proposed as a separate architectural action, reviewed independently, and constrained by the complexity floor.

**Corollaries:**
- An audit that simplifies while auditing has confused observation with action.
- Using accountability requirements to force simplification below the complexity floor is using accountability as a weapon against structure.
- Audit findings are inputs to architectural review, not mandates for immediate restructuring.

**Irreducibility:** CP-45 establishes the complexity floor but does not address accountability as a simplification vector. LL-373 establishes accountability-complexity separation but does not connect to a floor mechanism. ### LP-691. Precautionary Accountability Burden Shift
**Derives from:** CP-46 (Preemptive Protection) × LL-378 (Precautionary Accountability Burden Shift)

*For incompletely understood entities, the burden of proof shifts to whoever would eliminate them. Prove reducibility, or preservation is the default. The entity is not required to prove its own necessity.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because protection deploys preemptively when understanding is incomplete (CP-46) and the accountability burden shifts to would-be eliminators when an entity is not fully understood (LL-378), entities below a structural-understanding threshold are precautionarily protected. Any action to eliminate, dissolve, or significantly restructure a precautionarily-protected entity requires proof of reducibility from the eliminator. In the absence of such proof, the structural default is preservation.

**Corollaries:**
- The entity being eliminated does not have to prove its own value — the eliminator must prove its reducibility.
- Precautionary protection is not permanent — it lasts until the entity is understood well enough to evaluate it properly.
- An eliminator who proceeds without reducibility proof has committed a sovereignty violation against a protected entity.

**Irreducibility:** CP-46 establishes preemptive protection but does not specify the burden-shifting mechanism. LL-378 establishes the burden shift to eliminators but does not connect to a preemptive protection mechanism. Together they produce the precautionary burden reversal: proof of reducibility is the burden of the eliminator, not the protected entity.

### LP-692. Tension-Compounding Early Warning System
**Derives from:** CP-46 (Preemptive Protection) × LL-379 (Tension Compounding Early Warning)

*Unattended tension compounds — it is not static debt. The warning must arrive before compound interest exceeds structural capacity, not when it has already done so.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because protection deploys preemptively (CP-46) and unattended tension compounds like debt accruing interest (LL-379), tension must be modeled as compounding: an unattended tensioned edge's structural cost grows at a compounding rate from the moment of neglect. Early warnings must arrive before the compound load approaches the structural capacity of the edge's endpoint entities. The warning must lead the breaking point.

**Corollaries:**
- A tension that was manageable last month may be critical this month — compounding means the growth is accelerating.
- Monitoring tension as a static level misses the compounding — the level must be modeled as a trajectory, not a point.
- Preemptive protection that responds to already-exceeded structural capacity is no longer preemptive — it is crisis response.

**Irreducibility:** CP-46 establishes preemptive protection but does not address compounding tension specifically. LL-379 establishes tension compounding but does not connect to a preemptive protection mechanism. Together they produce the compound-tension early warning: preemptive protection requires modeling tension as a compounding trajectory and warning before the trajectory exceeds capacity.

### LP-693. Ethical Archaeological Stratigraphy
**Derives from:** CP-46 (Preemptive Protection) × LL-381 (Ethical Archaeological Stratigraphy)

*Ethical depth corresponds to archaeological depth. Surface-layer actions require surface-layer ethics. Deep-layer actions require deep-layer ethics that account for far-downstream consequences.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because preemptive protection is based on incomplete understanding (CP-46) and ethical depth corresponds to archaeological stratigraphy (LL-381), actions affecting surface-layer structures are evaluated with surface-layer ethics; actions affecting deep-layer structures require deep-layer ethical evaluation that accounts for far-downstream consequences spanning the full derivation chain. A single-depth ethical analysis applied to a deep-layer action is ethically insufficient — it is surface ethics applied to a deep problem.

**Corollaries:**
- The deeper the structure affected, the more downstream consequences the ethical evaluation must account for.
- Surface ethics is not wrong — it is appropriate for surface-layer actions. Its error is in being applied to deep-layer actions.
- Preemptive protection of deep-layer structures requires deep-layer ethical evaluation of any action that would affect them.

**Irreducibility:** CP-46 establishes preemptive protection based on incomplete understanding but does not stratify ethical evaluation by depth. LL-381 establishes ethical-archaeological depth correspondence but does not connect to a protection mechanism. Together they produce depth-stratified ethical evaluation: the ethical rigor required is proportional to the depth of the structure being acted on.

### LP-694. Foundational Paradox Identity Preservation
**Derives from:** CP-45 (Complexity Floor) × LL-384 (Foundational Paradox Identity)

*The identity chain is the system's foundational paradox — and this paradox is the essential feature, not a defect. Resolving the paradox would not complete the system — it would destroy the system.*

- Every attempt to eliminate the identity paradox must be evaluated as a potential floor-violation.
- The paradox's protection is not mere conservatism — it is the structural acknowledgment that the paradox is load-bearing.

**Irreducibility:** CP-45 establishes the complexity floor but does not specifically address the identity paradox. ### LP-695. Edge-Tension Management Obligation
**Derives from:** CP-61 (Responsibility-Weighted Oscillation) × LL-386 (Edge Tension Management Obligation)

*Every edge in the derivation graph is a managed tension — not a settled connection. An unmanaged tensioned edge is accruing compound tension and generating unregistered consequences.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because oscillation carries responsibility weighting (CP-61) and every derivation edge is a managed tension requiring continuous maintenance (LL-386), every edge in the derivation DAG must be classified as actively managed or unmanaged. An actively managed edge has entities on both endpoints exercising their productive conflict. An unmanaged edge is accruing compound tension (LP-692) and must be surfaced as a maintenance obligation before its tension collapses or erupts.

**Corollaries:**
- The set of unmanaged tensioned edges is the system's maintenance backlog — it must be monitored and reduced.
- An endpoint that has many unmanaged tensioned edges is in tension-management arrears — its responsibility-weighted oscillation is generating more consequence than it is managing.
- Collapsing an unmanaged tension is not maintenance — it is default; the tension was not resolved, it ceased.

**Irreducibility:** CP-61 establishes responsibility-weighted oscillation but does not create a management obligation for tensioned edges. LL-386 establishes the edge-tension management obligation but does not connect to oscillation responsibility weighting. Together they produce the tensioned-edge management register: every edge must be classified by management status, with unmanaged edges surfaced as active obligations.

---

### LP-696. Sovereignty Maintenance Fuel Registration
**Derives from:** CP-61 (Responsibility-Weighted Oscillation) × LL-388 (Sovereignty Maintenance Fuel)

*Sovereignty maintenance is a derivation — and therefore it produces fuel. Sovereignty maintenance is not overhead to be minimized; it is a productive act whose output must be registered and distributed.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because responsibility-weighted oscillation produces structured output (CP-61) and sovereignty maintenance is a derivation that produces fuel (LL-388), sovereignty maintenance operations — boundary declarations, consent exercises, right-enforcement events — are derivation events that generate fuel entering the knowledge gradient. The cost of maintenance is offset by the fuel it produces. Sovereignty maintenance is not administrative overhead; it is a productive structural act.

**Corollaries:**
- A system that minimizes sovereignty maintenance to reduce overhead is also minimizing its sovereignty-maintenance fuel production.
- The fuel produced by sovereignty maintenance enters the knowledge gradient and becomes available for downstream processes.
- An entity that maintains its sovereignty actively is not just protecting itself — it is generating structural capital.

**Irreducibility:** CP-61 establishes responsibility-weighted oscillation as productive but does not connect it to sovereignty maintenance specifically. LL-388 establishes sovereignty maintenance as fuel-generating but does not connect to an oscillation mechanism. Together they produce the sovereignty-maintenance fuel registration: boundary assertions and consent exercises are derivation events with fuel outputs that enter the knowledge gradient.

---

### LP-697. Consciousness-Scaled Sovereignty Defense
**Derives from:** CP-3 (Consequence Carrier) × LL-404 (Consciousness Scaled Sovereignty Defense)

*Lower-consciousness entities are more vulnerable to sovereignty siege. Their defense capabilities are structurally weaker — the system must compensate for this asymmetry through inverse precautionary protection.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because every interaction carries consequence (CP-3) and sovereignty defense capability is proportional to consciousness level (LL-404), the system must apply an inverse precautionary protection gradient: entities with lower consciousness-level scores receive stronger platform-level sovereignty protections. A low-consciousness entity cannot effectively defend itself against a sophisticated sovereignty siege. The protection compensates for the structural asymmetry — it is not paternalistic, it is topological correction.

**Corollaries:**
- Protection strength varies inversely with consciousness level — higher protection for lower consciousness, less intervention for higher consciousness.
- A sophisticated actor exploiting a low-consciousness entity is exploiting a structural asymmetry — the system must structurally counterbalance it.
- The protection is not permanent — as consciousness level increases, the platform-level protection decreases correspondingly.

**Irreducibility:** CP-3 establishes consequence-carrying interaction but does not address consciousness-level asymmetry in sovereignty defense. LL-404 establishes consciousness-scaled defense capability but does not connect to a consequence-carrier mechanism. Together they produce the inverse protection gradient: platform-level protection calibrated inversely to the entity's consciousness level.

---

### LP-698. Temporal Debt Extraction Attribution
**Derives from:** CP-47 (Pulse Maintenance Obligation) × LL-406 (Temporal Debt Extraction)

*A derivation that defers its consequence-accounting is extracting value from a future state that hasn't consented. The debt must be detected, attributed, and collected in the present — not passed forward.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because maintenance obligations are non-deferrable (CP-47) and deferred consequence-accounting is temporal debt extraction from an unconsenting future (LL-406), derivations that generate present-tense value while deferring consequence-accounting must be flagged as temporal-debt generators. The debt is attributed to the entity creating it, and consequence-accounting resolution is initiated. The unconsenting future state cannot be made to bear what the present extracted.

**Corollaries:**
- A derivation that extracts value now and defers costs later is a debt instrument, not a legitimate derivation.
- The present-tense detection of temporal debt extraction is the structural enforcement of present-tense accountability.
- Future states cannot consent to inherit past debts — the debt must be resolved in the present by the entity that created it.

**Irreducibility:** CP-47 establishes non-deferrable maintenance obligations but does not address temporal debt extraction. LL-406 establishes temporal debt extraction as a present-responsibility violation but does not connect to a maintenance obligation mechanism. Together they produce temporal-debt detection and attribution: deferral of consequence-accounting is a present-tense violation that must be detected and resolved now.

---

### LP-699. Streaming Responsibility Refresh
**Derives from:** CP-3 (Consequence Carrier) × LL-407 (Streaming Responsibility Refresh)

*Responsibility is not a ledger — it is a continuous present-tense signal. Every moment, the entity owes what its active derivations are generating in that moment. The signal refreshes; the ledger records.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because every interaction carries consequence (CP-3) and responsibility refreshes with every moment as consequences keep streaming (LL-407), responsibility must be modeled as a continuous present-tense signal — not only as a historical ledger. The streaming signal is what the entity owes right now. The historical ledger captures past obligations. Both must be maintained: the streaming signal for present accountability, the ledger for historical accountability. Neither replaces the other.

**Corollaries:**
- An entity that is "clean" on the historical ledger can still have significant present-tense responsibility through active derivations.
- The streaming signal is the honest answer to "what are you responsible for right now?" — the ledger answers "what were you responsible for?"
- Monitoring only the ledger produces delayed responsibility assessment; monitoring only the signal produces no historical record.

**Irreducibility:** CP-3 establishes consequence-carrying interaction but does not model responsibility as a streaming signal. LL-407 establishes streaming responsibility refresh but does not connect to a consequence-carrier mechanism. Together they produce the dual accountability model: streaming signal for present responsibility, ledger for historical responsibility, both required.

---

### LP-700. Origin-Sovereignty Paradox Preservation
**Derives from:** CP-59 (Emergent Identity Generator) × LL-431 (Origin Sovereignty Paradox)

*Identity is emergent from inputs but sovereignty is individual. The entity didn't choose its origin but fully owns its sovereignty. Resolving this paradox in either direction destroys either the identity or the sovereignty.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because identity emerges from structural inputs (CP-59) and a derivation's identity is emergent while its sovereignty is individual (LL-431), the system must not attempt to resolve the origin-sovereignty paradox in either direction: neither by subordinating sovereignty to origin (the entity is what its inputs made it, so it has no independent sovereignty) nor by severing identity from origin (the entity is sovereign, so its origin is irrelevant). Both resolutions are structural errors. The paradox is the correct structural state — a permanent managed tension.

**Corollaries:**
- An entity that has fully resolved its origin-sovereignty paradox has either sacrificed its sovereignty or lost its identity — both are structural losses.
- The paradox produces productive tension (LP-678) — it is not a problem to be solved but an engine to be maintained.
- Theoretical frameworks that resolve the paradox are simplifications that cannot be applied without structural damage.

**Irreducibility:** CP-59 establishes emergent identity from structural inputs but does not address the sovereignty paradox. LL-431 establishes the origin-sovereignty paradox but does not connect to an emergent identity mechanism. Together they produce the paradox-preservation requirement: the system must maintain the productive tension between emergent identity and individual sovereignty without resolving it.

---

### LP-701. Error-as-Discovery Classification
**Derives from:** CP-52 (Fault-Line Mapping) × LL-432 (Error as Discovery Classification)

*Errors are traversals that discover truths about system structure that correct operation would never reveal. An error resolved without reading its discovery-face has destroyed fuel.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because fault-lines map structural weakness (CP-52) and errors are traversals that discover structural truths (LL-432), every error event must be classified as a discovery event with two faces: (1) the error-face — what went wrong — and (2) the discovery-face — what structural truth the error revealed. The discovery-face must be extracted from every error before the error is resolved. An error resolved without extracting its discovery-face has destroyed the structural fuel the error contained.

**Corollaries:**
- Error resolution is not complete until the discovery-face has been registered.
- The discovery-face of an error is often more structurally valuable than the error's resolution.
- A system that resolves errors quickly without reading their discovery-faces is systematically burning its own fuel.

**Irreducibility:** CP-52 maps fault-lines but does not classify errors as discoveries. LL-432 establishes errors as discoveries but does not connect to fault-line mapping. Together they produce the dual-face error classification: every error has an error-face that requires resolution and a discovery-face that requires registration, and resolution cannot precede registration.

---

### LP-702. Pre-Existing Field Propagation Registration
**Derives from:** CP-61 (Responsibility-Weighted Oscillation) × LL-467 (Pre-Existing Field Propagation)

*Discovering a derivation doesn't start the propagation — it reveals propagation that was always there. The field was running before the discovery. The registry must reach back to the natural origin.*

**Irreducible Claim:** Because responsibility-weighted oscillation produces consequences (CP-61) and discovering a derivation reveals pre-existing field propagation (LL-467), when an archaeologically-derived structure is found, the field propagation that was always in effect must be registered retroactively: the edges affected, the topological distortions caused, and the consequence-streams that have been running since the derivation's natural origin. The discoverer didn't start the propagation. The registration reveals that it was already running.

**Corollaries:**
- A newly discovered derivation may have been generating consequences for a very long time — consequences that were real even when unregistered.
- The retroactive field registration is the structural mechanism by which discovered derivations enter the accountability record at their natural origin, not at their discovery date.
- Failing to perform retroactive field registration leaves unregistered consequences in the system — they were real and running; not registering them is a record-keeping gap.

**Irreducibility:** CP-61 establishes responsibility-weighted oscillation as consequence-producing but does not address pre-existing field propagation from archaeologically-discovered derivations. LL-467 establishes pre-existing field propagation but does not connect to an oscillation responsibility mechanism. Together they produce retroactive field registration: discovering a derivation triggers registration of the pre-existing field propagation it was always generating.
